G.S.R. 279(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by section 39 read with section 295 of the Income-tax 
Act, 96] (43 of I96l), the Central Board of Direct Taxes hereby makes the following rules further to amend 
Income-tax Rules, 962, namely: - 


l. (.) These rules may be called the Income-tax (Thirteenth Amendment) Rules, 2025; 
(2) They shall come into force with effect from the I* day of April, 2025; 
2. In the Income-tax Rules, [962 (hereinafter referred to as the said rules), in Appendix II, for FORM ITR-3,
